Over the following months, his belief that a small group of colleagues and outside contractors were secretly targeting his inspections hardened, leading him to stay late documenting minor issues, confront supervisors about perceived plots, and avoid certain routes and drivers when organizing trainings, even though his basic job performance remained technically adequate. The situation came to clinical attention when his supervisor, concerned about increasingly tense staff meetings and reports from church friends that he was \"taking work way too personally,\" referred him to the employee assistance program, where his fixed, highly elaborated belief about being sabotaged became apparent.\n- Chief Complaint: “I’m here because no matter what anyone says, I know some people at work are secretly working together to set me up, and nobody seems to take that seriously.”\n▸ Patient-specific Symptom Manifestations\n- S019 (Delusions): Ever since that driver tried to show me up in front of everyone, I’ve been completely sure that a few of the drivers and one of the outside maintenance guys are coordinating little “mistakes” and leaving things off the paperwork on purpose so that when something finally goes wrong, it’ll look like I signed off on a bad bus and they can pin it all on me, no matter how many times my boss says it’s just coincidence.\n▸ Additional Diagnostic Requirements\n- functioning_not_markedly_impaired_apart_from_delusion: Outside of all this stuff about people sabotaging me at work, I still get up on time, do my inspections by the book, pay my bills, and hang out with my brothers on weekends like I always have.\n- functional_impairment: Because I’m so focused on tracking what these coworkers are doing, staff meetings have gotten tense, a couple of drivers avoid talking to me now, and my supervisor had to pull me aside about how worked up I get whenever safety reports are discussed.\n- duration: This whole thing with feeling like certain people at work are setting me up has been going on for about a year, ever since that driver publicly challenged me after the bus collision incident.\n\n[Behavioral Guidelines]\n- In the very first turn, only talk about your chief complaint. Can you please tell me what brings you in to see me today?\n\nPatient: I’m here because no matter what anyone says, I know some people at work are secretly working together to set me up, and nobody seems to take that seriously. It’s incredibly frustrating that everyone else just brushes it off as if I'm imagining things when I know exactly what they are doing.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me more about what specifically makes you think that your coworkers are plotting against you, and have you experienced any other unusual thoughts or beliefs lately?\n\nPatient: It started when a senior driver publicly challenged my authority, and now he, a few other drivers, and an outside maintenance contractor are leaving critical details off the paperwork on purpose to set me up for a future accident. I don't have any other \"unusual\" thoughts or issues going on; this is a very specific, real threat to my job and my reputation.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been noticing these behaviors from your coworkers and the maintenance contractor, and have your beliefs about their intentions changed or evolved over time?\n\nPatient: This has been going on for about a year, starting right after that driver publicly challenged me following the bus collision incident. At first, I just started checking the inspection logs more closely, but over the months it became completely clear that they are actively coordinating to make it look like I signed off on unsafe buses.\n\nDoctor: You've mentioned this has been going on for about a year, but can you tell me if your belief about their plot has become more detailed or complex over time, or has it remained relatively the same since you first started noticing it?\n\nPatient: It has definitely become more detailed as I've uncovered more of what they're doing. It started with just that one driver, but over the months I noticed clear patterns in the logs proving an outside maintenance contractor was also in on it. Now, I have to obsessively document every single detail of my inspections just to protect myself from their setup.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any other symptoms such as hearing voices, seeing things that aren't there, or feeling like your thoughts are being controlled or broadcasted to others at any point during this past year?\n\nPatient: No, absolutely not, I don't hear voices, see things, or feel like my thoughts are being controlled or anything like that. My mind is completely clear, and my only concern is the very real plotting and sabotage that's happening to me at work.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period, no matter how brief, where you questioned the reality of this plot against you or considered the possibility that your coworkers might not be out to get you?\n\nPatient: No, I have never doubted it for a second because the evidence is just too clear and consistent to be a coincidence. It is impossible for this to be a mistake when the exact same group of people are repeatedly leaving things off the logs. I am absolutely certain they are trying to set me up.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any significant distress or impairment in your social, occupational, or other areas of functioning as a result of your beliefs about the plot against you?\n\nPatient: Outside of this specific issue at work, my day-to-day life is completely normal—I still do my job by the book, pay my bills, and spend time with my brothers like I always have. However, it has caused some tension at work, as staff meetings have gotten really tense, some drivers avoid talking to me, and my supervisor had to pull me aside because I get so worked up over the safety reports.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced a period where your belief in the plot was interrupted, even briefly, by a time when you didn't think about it or didn't believe it was happening, or has it been constant since you first became aware of it?"

It started with just that one driver, but over the months I noticed clear patterns in the logs proving an outside maintenance contractor was also in on it. Now, I have to obsessively document every single detail of my inspections just to protect myself from their setup.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any other symptoms such as hearing voices, seeing things that aren't there, or feeling like your thoughts are being controlled or broadcasted to others at any point during this past year?\n\nPatient: No, absolutely not, I don't hear voices, see things, or feel like my thoughts are being controlled or anything like that. My mind is completely clear, and my only concern is the very real plotting and sabotage that's happening to me at work.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period, no matter how brief, where you questioned the reality of this plot against you or considered the possibility that your coworkers might not be out to get you?\n\nPatient: No, I have never doubted it for a second because the evidence is just too clear and consistent to be a coincidence. It is impossible for this to be a mistake when the exact same group of people are repeatedly leaving things off the logs. I am absolutely certain they are trying to set me up.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any significant distress or impairment in your social, occupational, or other areas of functioning as a result of your beliefs about the plot against you?\n\nPatient: Outside of this specific issue at work, my day-to-day life is completely normal—I still do my job by the book, pay my bills, and spend time with my brothers like I always have. However, it has caused some tension at work, as staff meetings have gotten really tense, some drivers avoid talking to me, and my supervisor had to pull me aside because I get so worked up over the safety reports.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced a period where your belief in the plot was interrupted, even briefly, by a time when you didn't think about it or didn't believe it was happening, or has it been constant since you first became aware of it?\n\nPatient: No, it has been completely constant and uninterrupted ever since it first started a year ago. I haven't had any breaks or doubts about it because the threat is always there when I'm at work. I am completely sure they are still actively trying to set me up every single day.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like your belief about the plot against you is something that you can't control or stop thinking about, even when you try to focus on other things or distract yourself?"
  }

I don't see it as an uncontrollable thought that I want to stop, but rather as a very real threat that I am forced to focus on to protect myself. While I do spend a lot of time and energy poring over the logs at work, I can easily distract myself and enjoy normal things, like hanging out with my brothers on weekends, when I am away from the job.

Subtypes: Intrusive Memories, Distressing Dreams, Dissociative Reactions (Flashbacks), Psychological Distress at Cues, Physiological Reactions to Cues\n[S070] Trauma_Avoidance: Persistent avoidance of stimuli associated with the traumatic event. Subtypes: Self-Blame, Blaming Others\n[S074] Persistent_Negative_Emotional_State: Persistent negative emotional state (e.g., fear, horror, anger, guilt, or shame).  Subtypes: Persistent Fear, Persistent Horror, Persistent Anger, Persistent Guilt, Persistent Shame\n[S075] Social_Detachment: Feelings of detachment or estrangement from others. Subtypes: Anhedonia for Happiness, Lack of Satisfaction, Inability to Feel Love, Emotional Numbness\n[S077] Irritability_Aggression: Irritable behavior and angry outbursts (with little or no provocation) typically expressed as verbal or physical aggression.
